plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:195 -> CLAUDE.md:195: ### Code Quality - ZERO TOLERANCE FOR WARNINGS
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:196 -> CLAUDE.md:196: 
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:197 -> CLAUDE.md:197: **Every build must be completely clean.** Zero warnings, zero errors. This is non-negotiable.
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:198 -> CLAUDE.md:198: 
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:199 -> CLAUDE.md:199: When you run any build or test command and observe warnings or errors in the compile stage, you MUST fix them before proceeding. Do not continue with broken builds.
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:200 -> CLAUDE.md:200: 
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:201 -> CLAUDE.md:201: **Honest fixes only.** Do NOT suppress warnings dishonestly:
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:202 -> CLAUDE.md:202: - `#[allow(dead_code)]` is NOT acceptable for code that should be removed or actually used
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:203 -> CLAUDE.md:203: - `#[allow(unused_variables)]` is NOT acceptable for variables that indicate incomplete implementation
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:204 -> CLAUDE.md:204: - Prefixing with `_` is NOT acceptable if the variable was meant to be used
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:205 -> CLAUDE.md:205: - These annotations hide problems instead of fixing them
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:206 -> CLAUDE.md:206: 
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:207 -> CLAUDE.md:207: **When to use suppression attributes:**
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:208 -> CLAUDE.md:208: - `#[allow(dead_code)]` ONLY for legitimate public API functions that are intentionally available but not yet called (e.g., `SpinLock::try_lock()` as part of a complete lock API)
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:209 -> CLAUDE.md:209: - `#[cfg(never)]` for code intentionally disabled for debugging (must be in Cargo.toml check-cfg)
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:210 -> CLAUDE.md:210: - Never use suppressions to hide incomplete work or actual bugs
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:211 -> CLAUDE.md:211: 
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:212 -> CLAUDE.md:212: **Proper fixes:**
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:213 -> CLAUDE.md:213: - Unused variable? Either use it (complete the implementation) or remove it entirely
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:214 -> CLAUDE.md:214: - Dead code? Either call it or delete it
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:215 -> CLAUDE.md:215: - Unnecessary `mut`? Remove the `mut`
plan-citation-audit.txt CLAUDE.md:216 -> CLAUDE.md:216: - Unnecessary `unsafe`? Remove the `unsafe` block
